names one integer pencil pair, $(q^2E:p^2M)$, with its site datum
$S$ at the join $q^2E=p^2M+S$, and the two coordinates'
representatives are the datum's positive rescalings,
\[
q^2\,H\big([\,1:\tau^2\,]\big)\;=\;S\;=\;p^2\,H\big([\,\sigma:1\,]\big)
\qquad\text{at}\qquad q\,\tau=p,\quad p^2\sigma=q^2 ,
\]
so every subspace read agrees at rescaled levels: a
rescaling-fixed direct summand reads at rescaled levels, the
fibers among them (Lemma~\ref{lem:fiberdec}), and on each,
\[
\operatorname{count}_{[1:\tau^2]}(\lambda)
\;=\;\operatorname{count}_{S}(q^2\lambda)
\;=\;\operatorname{count}_{[\sigma:1]}(\sigma\lambda)
\]
at every level, the reversal count blind to the positive factors
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), brackets, jumps and extents rescaled by the one
factor with their levels. A stored level therefore names its
representative, and a seam comparison is two reads of the one integer
pencil at matched levels, the factors the display's.

\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The near-contact cell]\label{lem:contactcell}
The dual pencil, the pair $(\sigma E\!:\!M)$'s site datum
$P(\sigma)$, is affine in $\sigma$, and on a
fiber head (Lemma~\ref{lem:dualtrunc}) its entries are one
polynomial-pair list in $(t,\sigma)$ over the residue beyond the
locality band
(Lemma~\ref{lem:fiberdec}; Lemma~\ref{lem:stableentries}), the window entering
through its sampled momenta and its separation range alone, the residue a
further coordinate beyond the reach bound
(Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(iii); Lemma~\ref{lem:serstable}(iii)
at the further series). Every count of the dual chain is
therefore Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}'s cell function over
$(t,\sigma,r)$ with located divisors: the \emph{contact cell} is the
contact end's, its $\sigma$-extent $\sigma_1$ the divisor's first positive
root, an output of
Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(iii)'s reads, the residue direction the
classifications' (Lemma~\ref{lem:chaininstances};
Lemma~\ref{lem:serdivisors} at the further series), the divisors'
roots against the families' clearances; anchors at pair $\sigma$ are window
reads; and $r=1$ enters as a residue read like any other, $c_1=1$ flowing
through the channel data (Lemma~\ref{lem:channels}).

\emph{The truncation pair.} On the $X$-sector cell's chain (the
vacuum's adjoint closure, one label per index entry at the
electric read $4\hat c_2$, Construction~\ref{con:xfusion}) the
chain's count at a level is bracketed by two finite divisors. The
first is the plain truncation, a compression, its counts at or
below the chain's (Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}). The second is the
\emph{frontier-bordered} pencil: the head with one auxiliary row
per frontier label (a head label whose adjoint fusion leaves the
cutoff, Proposition~\ref{prop:fusionfinite}), coupling one and
auxiliary diagonal the pair
$[\,\langle 4\sigma c_+\!:\!d_\theta+\lambda\rangle:\varkappa_B\,]$,
its first member the seed floor
$F:=\langle 4\sigma c_+\!:\!d_\theta\rangle$ at the level on the
second member: $c_+$ is the least
Casimir beyond the cutoff among the frontier's targets, and $F$
floors the whole excluded block: an excluded label of the closure
is nonunit at the unit class (the $X$-sector's own,
Lemma~\ref{lem:chargedcell}(i)), so it moves, one move within
the adjoint stencil descending the Casimir
(Lemma~\ref{lem:casfloor}'s moves), the moved pair an occupied
row read, the fundamental against its complement collecting the
target's count through the associativity at the two one-box
rows, the full-column summand reading the sum's unit at the
moved target and one summand occupied, the withdrawn box's row
read against the added one's (Lemma~\ref{lem:pieri};
Lemma~\ref{lem:adjchar}; Construction~\ref{con:labels};
Lemma~\ref{lem:blockcount}(iii); Lemma~\ref{lem:dualread}(ii)),
and the moves chain at the strict descent
(Lemma~\ref{lem:casfloor}), each step's source a member of its
target's adjoint row, the chain's first head target inside the
degree window at the cleared read's own bound
(Proposition~\ref{prop:windowfinite}): every excluded label reads
a frontier target at or below its own Casimir, $c_+$ at or below
the whole block; $d_\theta$ is the
magnetic form cap, kept by every principal block
(Lemma~\ref{lem:fpcap}); and the \emph{border cap} $\varkappa_B$
is the product of the coupling's largest row and column sums,
$BB^{\mathsf T}\preceq\varkappa_B$ on the frontier
support, the two-sided Schur read. Between any two cutoffs the
excluded block sits at or above $F$ ($\sigma$ times its Casimir
floor against the magnetic cap), so at every level below $F$
eliminating it puts the larger head's read at or above the
bordered pencil's own Schur read, the auxiliary pivots positive
there and inertia additive (Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}): at every
level below $F$ the chain's count sits between the pair's two
counts at every cutoff, and a
level whose two counts agree is a located count of the chain
itself, the count beyond every cutoff
(Lemma~\ref{lem:dualtrunc}). At $r=2$ the
label lattice is two-row, a reduced shape at most $r$ rows deep
(Construction~\ref{con:labels}), and the head decimates by
Theorem~\ref{thm:decimation}(ii) at the dual representative, the deep
rows geometric by Lemma~\ref{lem:spectator}(i),(ii). A shell ordering of the
elimination is certificate data (Theorem~\ref{thm:decimation}(i)), its
validity divisors (the seed's dominance root and the envelope's own
positivity ceiling) entering the emitted list as any boundary's do;
the deep tower rows repeat off the collar (Lemma~\ref{lem:grading}),
the decimation's constant-stencil direction.

\emph{The contact end is interior to its cell.} At the contact end the head pencil
is the magnetic member's balance partner
(Proposition~\ref{prop:segment}), its level pair's datum the
balance partner of $M+\lambda G$, and the counts are fusion-matrix reads, the walk's own
divisor data (Theorem~\ref{thm:channeldiv}; Proposition~\ref{prop:row}): at a
level off that divisor the cell's determinant reads unequal members at
the contact end, so the contact end sits inside the contact cell and its counts are the cell's, direct
reads; at a level on the divisor, Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}'s boundary
clause extends the positive-semidefinite cells into the closed
endpoint.

\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The corner pencil]\label{lem:corner}
Toward the contact end the chain reads in the \emph{corner
coordinate}: the ray $[\,\eta^2:1\,]$ at the scale pair $\eta$,
its levels at the \emph{corner scale}, a level the product
$\eta\,\eta'$ at a \emph{height} $\eta'$, itself a balance pair
(Definition~\ref{def:ground}). The corner data are the dual
pencil's at the substitution $\sigma=\eta^2$,
$\lambda=\eta\,\eta'$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:dualtrunc}), and the corner
count is the reversal count of the form pair
\[
\operatorname{rev}\big(\,\eta^2E+\eta\,\eta'_-\,G\ :\
M+\eta\,\eta'_+\,G\,\big)
\qquad\text{at}\quad
\eta'=\langle\,\eta'_+\!:\!\eta'_-\,\rangle ,
\]
the height's members cross-added onto the pair's two sides
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}; Theorem~\ref{thm:certconstruct}), the
display cleared by the scale's square at the seam identity's
integer pencil (Lemma~\ref{lem:dualtrunc}), every entry's weight a
natural product of the scale's members and the height's. The
corner's \emph{shifted scaling}: at the magnetic form
cap $d_\theta$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:fpcap}) the shifted pencil
$Q(\sigma):=\sigma E+S_\theta$, at $S_\theta$ the pair
$(d_\theta\,G\!:\!M)$'s site datum, is a sum of two
positive-semidefinite forms (the electric diagonal's; the cap's,
Lemma~\ref{lem:fpcap}), and at scales $\sigma'<\sigma$ (two equal
scales reading one pencil outright) with the cofactor $\rho$
at $\rho\,\sigma=\sigma'$,
\[
\rho\,Q(\sigma)\ \preceq\ Q(\sigma')\ \preceq\ Q(\sigma) ,
\qquad
\rho\,\mu_j(\sigma)\ \le\ \mu_j(\sigma')\ \le\
\mu_j(\sigma) ,
\]
$\mu_j$ the shifted roots, $Q(\sigma')$ the sum
$\rho\,Q(\sigma)+w\,S_\theta$ at the gap $w$, the scales' order's
witness at $\rho+w=1$, read
against its second summand positive semidefinite, the root
reads the comparison's counts
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), cutoff-free and window-free, a form
comparison compressing. The ground's electric weight rides the
\emph{contact drift}: at the dual representative the ground identity
reads
\begin{gather*}
\sigma\,\langle\psi,E\,\psi\rangle
\;=\;\varepsilon_0(\sigma)\,\langle\psi,G\,\psi\rangle
+\langle\psi,M\,\psi\rangle ,\\
\sigma\,\langle\psi,E\,\psi\rangle\ \le\
g\,\langle\psi,G\,\psi\rangle
\quad\text{at the drift $g$ at }\
\varepsilon_0(\sigma)+m_+=g ,
\end{gather*}
the magnetic read at or below the top root $m_+$ of $(M\!:\!G)$ on
the head, the contact end's ground level the top root's
balance partner (Lemma~\ref{lem:split}; the
contact end's reads Lemma~\ref{lem:contactcell}'s), the order
witnessed by the counts monotone in the scale
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}): the electric weight is priced by two
located roots, the drift its own cap, and the scale sandwich's
electric weights read it at the grounds' own data
(Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(vii)). The drift obeys a
positive-rate cap, the \emph{upper family at the contact end}: on the
$X$-sector chain's head at the scale key, at a natural $N$ and a
pair $\xi$ at the gap $w$ at $\xi+w=1$, the two comparisons at
$N$ (the weighted power
$2\,d_\theta A^2(2N{+}1)^{2p}\,\xi^{4N}$ at or below $w$ at the
count reads $A$, $p$, and the grade reach $c_r\,(4N^2+1)$ inside
the scale key's cutoff) certificate data. The \emph{grade
key} is the highest root's coroot pair,
$G(\lambda):=\lambda(\theta^\vee)$, the interface's root coroot
pair at $\theta$ (Construction~\ref{con:gentable}), one natural
per label: at the fundamental fold $\lambda=\sum_im_i\,\omega_i$
the key is the dual fold's read, $G=\sum_im_i\,c^\vee_i$ at the
halved summands
$2\,c^\vee_i=c_i\,\langle\alpha_i,\alpha_i\rangle$ of the
residue fold, naturals at one or beyond joining to
$\sum_ic^\vee_i=r$ (Construction~\ref{con:sertables}), so the
coefficient sum sits at or below $G$, the vacuum reads the key
at the sum's unit, and $\rho$ reads it at $r$, the simple coroot
reads one each. Three reads price the family at the key.
\emph{The move cap}: a $\theta$ content is the unit content or a
root with its balance partner, the block's list at top $\theta$
(Construction~\ref{con:fusion}'s content list;
Lemma~\ref{lem:adjchar}; Proposition~\ref{prop:row}), each
content at or below the top (Lemma~\ref{lem:lowerspan}), the
join $\nu+\sum_id_i\,\alpha_i=\theta$ at natural $d_i$ reading
$\langle\nu,\theta\rangle\le\langle\theta,\theta\rangle=2$
against $\theta$'s dominant reads (Lemma~\ref{lem:tops}(ii)) at
the halved products, with the balance partner's read the further
side: $\nu(\theta^\vee)$ sits at magnitude at most two, and the
key moves by at most two under adjoint fusion. \emph{The Casimir
cap}: the fundamental display (Lemma~\ref{lem:casfloor}) reads
\[
C_2(\lambda)\;=\;\sum_{i,j}m_i\,m_j\,
\langle\omega_i,\omega_j\rangle
+\sum_im_i\,\langle\omega_i,2\rho\rangle\ ,
\]
every summand capped at the member's fundamental reads against
the coefficient sums, each at or below $G$: at a fixed member
the reads are the adjugate rows' displayed folds
(Construction~\ref{con:sertables}), and at a series member the
rows' magnitudes sit at or below the key (the sorted
coordinates: the key telescopes over the coroot presentation's
consecutive gaps to the top pair's sum with the last coordinate
withdrawn at $B_\ell$, the top coordinate at $C_\ell$, and the
top pair's sum with the next-to-last coordinate withdrawn at
$D_\ell$, each withdrawn coordinate at or below the kept pair's
second member at the sorted order, the last magnitude below its
neighbor at $D_\ell$), the coordinate
squares and the displayed key reads folding to the cap: $\hat
c_2(\lambda)$ sits below $c_r\,(G^2+1)$ at one cleared member
pair $c_r$. \emph{The counts}: the labels at one
key value are the dual fold's solutions, at most $(G+1)$ per
simple key, and the member gap product's factors read at or
below the $\theta$ factor,
$\mathrm{dot}(\lambda{+}\rho,\alpha)\le
\mathrm{dot}(\lambda{+}\rho,\theta)$ at the join's read against
$\lambda{+}\rho$'s coroot pairs with
$(\lambda{+}\rho)(\theta^\vee)=G+r$, so the per-key label and
dimension counts sit below $A\,(G+1)^{p}$ at member reads
$c_r$, $A$, $p$ (Corollary~\ref{cor:weyldim};
Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(i) with
Lemma~\ref{lem:serstable}(i) at the further series, the fixed
members' reads the adjugate rows'). The \emph{dimension-weighted
family} is
\[
\phi_\xi\;:=\;\sum_{G(\lambda)\le 2N} d_\lambda\,
\xi^{G(\lambda)}\,\chi_\lambda ,
\]
the sum over the chain's labels at the grade key, the characters
orthonormal (Proposition~\ref{prop:repring}) and the vacuum's
term reading one; at the $A$-series the key reads the reduced
shape's top row, the pair
$\langle\lambda_1\!:\!\lambda_{d_f}\rangle$ at the reduced
shape's one-member site with the full column's read of equal
members (Construction~\ref{con:labels}), one value per label.
The move cap prices the magnetic read termwise at the dimension
eigen-identity
$\sum_\mu N^\mu_{\theta\nu}\,d_\mu=d_\theta\,d_\nu$
(Lemma~\ref{lem:fpcap}), every summand a natural at pair weights
with the key's weight falling as the key rises:
\[
\langle\phi_\xi,\,M\,\phi_\xi\rangle+B
\;\ge\;\xi^{2}\,d_\theta\,\langle\phi_\xi,\phi_\xi\rangle ,
\]
$B$ the top two key values' boundary term at the cap $d_\theta$,
the two keys' part of the pairing priced at the counts' squares,
below $w$ times the vacuum's term at the weighted power
comparison. The
electric read prices at the key's caps, $\hat c_2(\lambda)$
below $c_r\,(G^2+1)$ with the per-key label and dimension
counts below $A\,(G+1)^{p}$, the pairing's per-key weight the
fold of the dimensions' squares at or below the count's own square
$A^2(G+1)^{2p}$, and the weighted geometric sums
close on two displayed reads. The power cap is one rising-product
read,
\[
(L+1)^{\,j}\ \le\ (L+1)(L+2)\cdots(L+j)\ ,
\]
every factor $L+i$ at or beyond $L+1$; and the graded sums at the
rising-product weights,
$S_j(M):=\sum_{L\le M}(L+1)\cdots(L+j)\,x^{L}$ at $x+y=1$, descend
by Pascal's identity at the rising products,
$(L+1)\cdots(L+j+1)=L\cdots(L+j)+(j+1)\,(L+1)\cdots(L+j)$, the
exact displays
\[
y\,S_{j+1}(M)+(M+1)\cdots(M+j+1)\,x^{M+1}\ =\ (j+1)\,S_j(M)\ ,
\qquad
y\,S_0(M)+x^{M+1}\ =\ 1\ ,
\]
so $y^{\,j+1}S_j(M)\le j!$ at every $M$. At $x=\xi^{2}$ the gap reads
$y=w\,(1+\xi)$ at or beyond $w$, the vacuum's unit term floors the
pairing, and
\[
w^{\,2p+3}\,\langle\phi_\xi,\,E\,\phi_\xi\rangle
\;\le\;4\,c_r\,A^2\,(2p+2)!\ \langle\phi_\xi,\phi_\xi\rangle\ :
\]
the rate's data are $q=2p+3$ and $C_E=4\,c_r\,A^2\,(2p+2)!$,
member reads.
The Rayleigh read at $\phi_\xi$ then closes the drift's rate
against the head's top root $m_+$ at its cap $d_\theta$
(Lemma~\ref{lem:fpcap}): at the scale's $w$, the bracket
$w^{\,q+1}\le\sigma\le(2w)^{\,q+1}$ the certificate's own read,
one power comparison,
\[
g(\sigma)^{\,q+1}\;\le\;C_g^{\,q+1}\,\sigma ,
\]
one cross-multiplied comparison per scale at the collected read
$C_g=2^{\,q+1}C_E+2\,d_\theta+1$, the Rayleigh read joining the
scale's electric power at the bracket's upper side to the boundary
and gap reads with the top root's cap at the drift, a located
$C_g$ per member and direction count: the contact drift falls at a
certified positive rate, the halving scales' ground-level widths
of the scale sandwich then geometric over the tail at the
contact-drift display's weights, their sum one cofactor read
(Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(vii)); and the tail's count read is the
corner disconjugacy certificate's
(Lemma~\ref{lem:cornerpivot}). On a fiber
head the entries are Lemma~\ref{lem:contactcell}'s list at the
substitution, one polynomial-pair list in $(t,\eta,\eta')$ over the
residue beyond the locality
band, so every corner count is Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}'s cell
function over the $(t,\eta,\eta')$ base with located boundaries,
and the \emph{corner divisor} is Theorem~\ref{thm:divisorid}'s
crossing read at that base. The \emph{corner cell} is the contact end's,
and its data are two located outputs per member and
direction count, each with its leading-term radius
(Theorem~\ref{thm:windowsep}; Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(iii);
Construction~\ref{con:lattice}'s direction reads): the
\emph{extent} $\eta_1$, the corner divisor's first positive root
in the scale over the cell, and the \emph{floor} $\kappa$, the
flat step's height extent on it (Theorem~\ref{thm:flatstep}). The
ray coordinate reads the outputs back at squares: the ray
$[\,a:b\,]$ sits in the extent exactly at
$a\,d_1^{\,2}\le n_1^{\,2}\,b$ at $\eta_1=[\,n_1\!:\!d_1\,]$, and
a level reads through its height, the cofactor at
$\eta\,\eta'=\lambda$ (Definition~\ref{def:ground}), the height
against the floor one pair comparison: the level's square against
the weight product, the two end weights' own scale.

\emph{The boundary families.} The corner cell's boundaries are the
located roots of four families over the $(t,\eta,\eta')$ base, each
family one of the chain's comparisons read at the substitution and
entering the list as any boundary does (Theorem~\ref{thm:decimation}, the
emitted record): the \emph{jump family}, the corner divisor's own
roots at the bracket's heights; the \emph{frontier family}, the
decimated envelope's dominance comparison at its seed, the frontier
Casimir's $\eta^2$-weight against the tail weight, a height at
which the two envelope counts agree a located count of the chain
itself (Lemma~\ref{lem:contactcell}'s decimated presentation;
Lemma~\ref{lem:dualtrunc}), so the cutoffs enter through the
envelope pair alone, and the scale keys its cutoff: $\Lambda(\eta)$
is the least natural at
$4\,\eta^{2}\,\Lambda\ge d_\theta+\lambda_{+}+\vartheta$, read over
the window's top level $\lambda_{+}$ at a clearance pair
$\vartheta$, one comparison per scale, the seed floor
$F=\langle 4\sigma c_{+}\!:\!d_\theta\rangle$ then clearing every
window level by
$\vartheta$ at once ($c_{+}\ge\Lambda$, the frontier's least Casimir
beyond the cutoff), so the envelope pair brackets the chain's count
at every scale of the tail together, the per-scale cutoff quantifier
riding the one key read, the key's comparison its own witness at
every member, $d_\theta$ the member's dimension fold with the
$A$-series' $r\,(r+2)$ its read (Proposition~\ref{prop:row}), and the
agreement heights' divisors the jump family's own
(Lemma~\ref{lem:dualtrunc}); the \emph{deck family}, the bulk blocks'
crossing loci with the graph read's side and the spectator caps'
three counts, each a cell datum of the corner base
(Lemma~\ref{lem:spectator}(iii); Lemma~\ref{lem:deckfactor}), the
$r=1$ reads among them, and the contraction cap read on the cell
at the boundary root's isolation datum: the cell is a component of
the divisor list's complement, the located root's bracket its
margin, and the cap's margin squares to the crossing read,
$\langle1\!:\!z^2\rangle^2=z^2\,\langle w^2\!:\!4\rangle$
(Lemma~\ref{lem:deckfactor}), the walk's mass point at the
crossing pair $[\,9:4\,]$ at base two
(Theorem~\ref{thm:channeldiv}); and the \emph{tension family}, the charged
sectors' tension, the winding shift's dressed floor against the
unit class's dressing folds, and the separated sectors' clearance
at the height floor
(Lemma~\ref{lem:chargedcell}(iii),(iv);
Lemma~\ref{lem:relfiber}(iii)), one root list per charge vector.
Every window read sits in the list's cells: the momenta are
sampled in $t$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:fiberdec}), the slabs read within
the spectator brackets (Theorem~\ref{thm:decimation}(iii)), the cutoffs
within the envelope pair, and the sectors at their families; so
the extent and the floor are outputs of the one list, and the
cell's $t$-section at the outputs is the whole range exactly where
the list's $t$-roots sit off it, itself a located read. The rank
direction inherits: the substitution fixes every entry's residue
dependence, so the families' divisors are the chain's own read at
corner base points, the deck base the member-key count, one value at every cleared
residue (Proposition~\ref{prop:row};
Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(ii)).

\emph{The cell floor.} The flat step's height extent on the corner
cell is a positive located datum of the cell: the ground root is simple over the cell (Theorem~\ref{thm:SO};
Theorem~\ref{thm:divisorid}(iii)), the second jump is separated at
Theorem~\ref{thm:windowsep}'s floor, and the counting functions
are flat between them at the certificate's two lines
(Lemma~\ref{lem:cornerpivot}). The heights read two-sided on the
cell: the lower side the floor's clearance, the upper the
truncation's second root against the bordered pencil's ground, the
compression's counts at or below the chain's and the bordered
pencil's at or above (Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}; the truncation
pair, Lemma~\ref{lem:contactcell}), the two root bounds
exchanging; an extent interval closes at its mixed window, the
ground's read at the interval's top with the second's at its
bottom, counts monotone in the coordinate
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), so the window is flat across the
interval and the height clears the interval's floor there, per
member and direction count. Each cell reads its edges at word
images: the flat window's upper end is the cell's second root, a
point of its own divisor (Lemma~\ref{lem:split};
Theorem~\ref{thm:divisorid}(i)), and the window cut's edge there,
where one is named, is the compressed pair's least root, read at a
word image with the two moments at equality and the first
positive, the state the word sector's own
(Lemma~\ref{lem:attained}(i),(ii)), one word image per window read
on the cell at the corner scale.
The extent's positivity is
the contact end's interiority read, the $\sigma_1$ brackets
Lemma~\ref{lem:contactcell}'s own outputs, and the floor's is the
certificate's, the rates derived and the gap a cut member's read,
positive by its shape (Lemma~\ref{lem:cornerpivot}(v),(vi)), the rank
direction at the key's bracket and the families' clearances
(Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(iii)).

\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The corner disconjugacy certificate]\label{lem:cornerpivot}
On a band-one head at the dual representative, at a scale pair
$\eta$ and a probe level cross-added in the diagonal
(Theorem~\ref{thm:certconstruct}'s spelling), the count
below the probe is the pivot sequence's lower-side count
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), and the read \emph{at most one} is
settled by four displayed comparisons at the corner scale on the
depth axis: the entries are
polynomials in the scale and the product $m\,\eta$ (the corner
scale's multiples, a pair read each), and every comparison below is a two-variable polynomial
read of those data.

\emph{(i) The pivot comparison.} At a positive list $v$ on a
segment with the termwise read
\[
v_{m}+v_{m+2}\ \le\ a_{m+1}\,v_{m+1}
\]
and the entry read $p_{m_0}v_{m_0}\ge v_{m_0+1}$, every later pivot
of the segment clears its entry read, $p_m\,v_m\ge v_{m+1}$: the pivot
identity $p_{m+1}\,p_m+1=a_{m+1}\,p_m$ with the two reads
$a_{m+1}\,v_{m+1}\ge v_m+v_{m+2}$ and $p_m\,v_m\ge v_{m+1}$ drives
the induction, one cross-multiplied step per depth.

\emph{(ii) The segment supersolutions are stated polynomial
profiles.} A segment's list enters as one polynomial $V$ in the
scale and the depth's two pair reads $m\,\eta$ and $m^{2}\eta$,
read per depth, $v_m=V(\eta,\,m\eta,\,m^{2}\eta)$: the termwise
comparison of (i) is then one polynomial read in the depth and the
scale, its positivity on the segment closed by the leading-term
radius in the depth (Theorem~\ref{thm:windowsep}) with the
finitely many depths below the radius each one read, and the
well's data enter at the square scale, where the probe line
$g(\eta)$ meets the band read $2$ at a stated pair rate and the
comparison's polynomials read the scale through the pair reads
alone. The profiles are the recurrence's own jets: at the probe
line's rate $q$ the diagonal reads $a_m=2+\eta\,W$ at the
\emph{well read}, the balance pair
\[
W\;=\;\big\langle\,[\,4:r+1\,]\,(s+r\,u)\,:\,q\,\big\rangle ,
\qquad u=m\,\eta,\quad s=m^{2}\eta ,
\]
its first member the tower's own electric read,
$[\,4:r+1\,]\,(s+r\,u)=4\,\eta\,\hat c_2(m\theta)$ at the
substitution (Proposition~\ref{prop:anchor}),
the neighbor fold has one scale factor,
$v_{m}+v_{m+2}=2v_{m+1}+\eta\,D[V]$ at the
displayed action
\[
m^{\,j}+(m{+}2)^{\,j}
\;=\;2\,(m{+}1)^{\,j}+2\sum_{i\ge1,\ 2i\le j}\binom{j}{2i}\,(m{+}1)^{\,j-2i}
\]
read per monomial of the pair coordinates, so (i)'s comparison is
the polynomial read $D[V]\le W\,V$; the jet solves $D[V]=W\,V$
degree by degree, the leading action mapping $u^{a}s^{b+1}$ to
$2\binom{a+2b+2}{2}\,u^{a}s^{b}$ with the $u$-pure monomials its
kernel, so each coefficient is one pair formula in the rate and the
residue and the jet's $u$-pure coefficients sit at its
seed alone, uniqueness by construction; the stated-degree jet is a
supersolution exactly where the remainder $W\,V_N-D[V_N]$ and the
jet read positive on the segment, two located divisor reads at the
stated degree, the degree certificate data held at its statement.
The constant-floor instance is the walk's own list, the
recurrence $U_{j}+U_{j+2}=g_i\,U_{j+1}$ at the boundary's unit seed
$U_1=1$ read from the
boundary (the truncation data of Theorem~\ref{thm:channeldiv}'s
recursion, integer-polynomial in the floor); seams glue at one
entry read each.

\emph{(iii) The crossing, and the rebound.} One located depth
reads its pivot on the lower side; the following pivot exceeds
its diagonal, the identity at a lower-side predecessor read on the
exceeding side (the rebound read), and seeds the later
segments' entry reads at the diagonal itself.

\emph{(iv) The dominance tail.} At depths whose diagonal clears
$2$ by a stated margin the constant list is the supersolution, and
the pivots stay clear to the head's end.

\emph{(v) The ground witness, and the lower line as the family's
own read.} The witness family is $x_m=s\,\langle s_2\!:\!s\rangle$
at the square-scale pair $s_2=N^{2}\eta$, the \emph{endpoint
depth} $N$ the least natural beyond one at
$40\,N^{4}\eta^{2}\ge363\,(r+1)$, one key read per scale, the
comparison the rate's two top terms' equality below; the family's
read at the endpoint is equal members and beyond it the sum's
unit. The form read telescopes at unit bonds, the
boundary bond's square $x_1^{2}$ the displayed first tail term,
\[
\sum_{m\ge1} a_m x_m^{2}
\;=\;2\sum_{m\ge1} x_m x_{m+1}
+x_1^{2}+\sum_{m\ge1}\langle x_{m+1}\!:\!x_m\rangle^{2}
+\eta\sum_{m\ge1}W_{-}\,x_m^{2}\ ,
\]
$W_{-}$ the lower line's well read, the bond pair per depth the
displayed square
$(2u+\eta)^{2}\,\langle s_2\!:\!2s+2u+\eta\rangle^{2}$, and each sum is one closed
read of the depth's powers at the endpoint (the power sums'
binomial identities), the closed reads' top coefficients at the
endpoint's powers $[\,8:315\,]$ at the family's square, degree
nine, $[\,44:105\,]$ at the difference sum, degree seven, and
$[\,4:r+1\,]\,[\,8:693\,]$ at the well sum's square part, degree
eleven. The \emph{lower rate} is the family's own cleared read,
\[
q_{-}\,\eta\sum_{m\ge1}x_m^{2}
\;=\;[\,4:r+1\,]\,\eta^{2}\sum_{m\ge1}(m^{2}+r\,m)\,x_m^{2}
+\sum_{m\ge1}\langle x_{m+1}\!:\!x_m\rangle^{2}+x_1^{2}\ ,
\]
one pair per scale and residue, the cofactor at the closed reads
(Definition~\ref{def:ground}), its two top terms $[\,33:2\,]$ at
the cofactor against $\eta N^{2}$ and $[\,20:11\,(r+1)\,]$ at the
factor $\eta N^{2}$, the tops' cofactor reads, whose equality is
the key's comparison, $40\,N^{4}\eta^{2}=363\,(r+1)$: at the line
$2+q_{-}\eta$ the form read's two sides read equal members at the
family, a positive-definite read excluded, so the least root sits
at or below the line (Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia};
Lemma~\ref{lem:split}), the family its own witness. The
second root prices from above by the pair read: at a cap line and
a second stated family $x'$ of the witness's shape, the span's
compressed pencil is one $2\times2$ read whose entries are
power-sum reads of the two lists, and a compression's counts sit at or below the
full form's (Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), so a compressed count of
two at the cap line places two chain roots below it: the second
root below the cap, the heights two-sided on the cell.

\emph{(vi) The probe lines, and the floor.} The lower rate is the
witness family's own read ((v)); an upper rate is a multiple,
$q_{+}=\mu\,q_{-}$ at a pair $\mu=[\,a:b\,]$ beyond one, and the
\emph{multiple cut} is the pairs at the count's read, at most one
below the line $2+\mu\,q_{-}\eta$: the counting function is
monotone in its level (Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), so the cut is
downward closed toward one, and membership at a member is settled
by (i)--(iv)'s reads at its line, a member's reads its whole
verification. The ground sits at or below the lower line by (v),
so the counting function is flat from the lower line to a
member's, and the rates' gap, $o$ at $q_{-}+o=\mu\,q_{-}$, is the
read $o=[\,g:b\,]\,q_{-}$ at the member's gap $g$ at $b+g=a$,
positive by its shape: the gap clears $o\,\eta$ at every
tail scale and the height's square clears $o^{\,2}$, one
cross-multiplication per scale, the floor per member and
direction count at every pair of the cut. The residue direction is a coordinate: the rates are
reads of the key's power sums, so the comparisons' polynomials
take the residue as a coordinate at the key's bracket
(Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(iii)), their roots read against the
families' clearances, one symbolic read at every residue at the
cleared families, and the channel-degeneration residues read the
walk's own displayed degenerations, the mass point at the crossing
among them (Theorem~\ref{thm:channeldiv}; Lemma~\ref{lem:channels}).

\emph{(vii) The block chain.} On a decimated head's block chain
(Theorem~\ref{thm:decimation}(i); Lemma~\ref{lem:greenprod}) the
certificate runs at block data, the band-one clauses its scalar
instance: the count splits over the depth pivots
(Lemma~\ref{lem:greenprod}(ii)); a segment's pivots stay positive
at the ball certificate of Lemma~\ref{lem:spectator}(iii) read at
per-depth stated centers, one matrix polynomial in the scale and
the depth's pair reads, the three counts cleared by the centers'
determinants to polynomial comparisons and read as cell functions
over the corner base (Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}), the center
defect the drift's slot; the crossing is one block's split at one
lower-side unit with its witness list certificate data
(Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}), and the rebound is the split's
rank-one read, the following pivot at or beyond its diagonal less
the positive part's transfer, one congruence identity and one
comparison; the dominance tail reads at block diagonal dominance;
and the ground witness and the cap pair read lift through the
compression unchanged ((v); Lemma~\ref{lem:inertia}). The
collar's finitely many blocks read exactly, the bonds enter
through their squares alone, and the certificate's data gain the
center family with its pairs and the crossing split.

The certificate's data are a cut member $\mu$, the segment list
with each boundary the cross-multiplied pair comparison of
$m^{2}\eta$ against a stated pair, the jets' stated degrees, the
crossing depth, and per segment and seam finitely many polynomial
comparisons in the scale and the scaled depth on stated boxes,
their reads located divisor data, the rates and the witness pair
(v)'s derived reads at the endpoint key
(Theorem~\ref{thm:windowsep}; Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}): the
well's oscillation reads at the corner scale, one read at every
scale, so one segment list runs the whole tail, and the one
certificate settles the count at most one at every depth and every
tail scale at once, the flat window's lower end the ground
bracket's own (Theorem~\ref{thm:SO}).

\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The moment folds]\label{lem:momentfold}
On a band-one head at the dual representative, at the ground's
recurrence $b_{m}\psi_{m}+b_{m+1}\psi_{m+2}=A_{m+1}\psi_{m+1}$ (the
eigen-identity's row read, the diagonal at the ground root
cross-added), the ground enters every datum read through two
moment streams,
\[
\rho[P]\;:=\;\sum_m \psi_m^{2}\,P(m)\ ,
\qquad
c[P]\;:=\;\sum_m \psi_m\,\psi_{m+1}\,P(m)\ ,
\]
$P$ over the polynomial weights, and the streams solve the
recurrence's own folds, exact at every scale:

\emph{(i) The fold identities.} Folding the recurrence against
$\psi_m P(m)$ reads
\[
c\big[\,b\,P\,\big]+c\big[\,b\,P^{+}\,\big]
\;=\;\rho\big[\,A\,P\,\big]
\qquad(P^{+}(m):=P(m{+}1))\ ,
\]
and the fold against $\psi_{m+1}P(m)$ reads a second identity of
the recurrence at the
shifted weights, its second-bond term reduced by one further fold
of the recurrence: every correlator beyond the two streams
re-enters them, so the family closes on $\rho$ and $c$ alone, one
identity per weight, each a display of the recurrence at a
polynomial weight. A commutator's ground read is of equal members
outright
(a commutator of two symmetric operators' pairing at a
ground reads equal members), which is why the folds rather than
commutators close the system.

\emph{(ii) The graded system, and its clearance.} At the corner
the weights read in the pair coordinates ($u=m\eta$,
$s=m^{2}\eta$): the diagonal $A_m$ reads $\sigma E_m$ at
weight-degree two, so each fold relates the streams' degree-$k$
reads to degree-$(k{+}2)$ at one scale factor, and the identities
at weights through a stated degree, with the eigenvalue's bracket
(the certificate's two lines, Lemma~\ref{lem:cornerpivot}(vi))
and the unit read $\rho[1]$ at the gram, are one linear system
for the truncated moment vector over the located stage, its
entries polynomial in the scale and the rates: the solve is the
elimination's (Definition~\ref{def:elim}), and the system's
determinant clearance is one located divisor read over the corner
base (Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}), the validity cell the
certificate's architecture.

\emph{(iii) The truncation cap.} Beyond the stated degree the
streams' tails read in the dominance region, where the ground's
components ride the transfer factors
(Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(ii)): the beyond-degree weight is
priced by the certificates' geometric caps against the turning
read, one comparison at the stated degree, the degree held at its
statement.

\emph{(iv) The scale comparison.} Two scales' systems share their
shape with coefficients differing by displayed polynomial reads of
the scale, so on the clearance cell the two moment vectors differ
by the elimination's cofactor reads at those differences, one
width per moment, and the widths take the scale factor: the
streams' reads close across the scales on the clearance cell.

\emph{(v) The datum assembly.} A single-region datum read is a
stream read at banded weights (Lemma~\ref{lem:stableentries}), so
its scale closure is (iv)'s on the clearance cell; separated and
Euclidean data assemble from single-region reads at the crossing
certificates (Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(iv)--(vi)), the
certificates cell data (Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}), so the
assembled reads close where the parts do. The block chains read
the streams at block weights through the folds of their own
recurrence, Lemma~\ref{lem:greenprod}(iii)'s row read.
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The cone read]\label{lem:cone}
The jump family's momentum structure at the corner is isotropic at
degree two: on the decimation's validity cells the decimated determinant's
root locus is the fiber symbol's own, one Schur factor at a
complement of unequal-membered determinant
(Theorem~\ref{thm:divisorid}(i)); the fiber
symbol's coefficients are permutation-invariant polynomials of the
chord list at every coupling of the approach
(Theorem~\ref{thm:restoration}, at Lemma~\ref{lem:corner}'s base),
their momentum reading even in each coordinate at the deck
relation's exchanged roots (Definition~\ref{def:deck};
Theorem~\ref{thm:restoration});
the degree-two invariants at that evenness are the standard
square's span
(Theorem~\ref{thm:restoration}, degree $2$ at the rings'
coincidence);
and the direction data enter at degree four, the breaking
multiplicities positive there. So the jump family's loci at the
corner read the isotropic square at degree two with one located
coefficient per root, the root reads Lemma~\ref{lem:stage}'s
at Theorem~\ref{thm:windowsep}'s separations, and the direction
reads two momentum degrees down.
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The free cell]\label{lem:freecell}
The pencil at the $\alpha{=}1$ representative, the pair
$(E\!:\!\tau^2M)$'s site datum $H([\,1:\tau^2\,])$, is affine in
$\tau^2$, and on a fiber head (Theorem~\ref{thm:truncation}) its entries
are one polynomial-pair list in $(t,\tau)$ over the residue beyond
the locality band
(Lemma~\ref{lem:fiberdec}; Lemma~\ref{lem:stableentries}), the window
entering through its sampled momenta and its separation range alone,
the residue a further coordinate beyond the reach bound
(Lemma~\ref{lem:rankstable}(iii); Lemma~\ref{lem:serstable}(iii)
at the further series): every count near the free end is
Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}'s cell function over $(t,\tau,r)$ with
located divisors, and the \emph{free cell} is the free end's, its
$\tau$-extent the divisor's first positive root, an output, the
contact cell's construction at this end (Lemma~\ref{lem:contactcell}).

\emph{The free end is interior to its cell.} At the free end the
pencil is its electric member $E$,
diagonal at the free reads: the decimated symbol's jumps are the occupied
contents and the level is the member floor label's loop read, one
diagonal computation at every window (Lemma~\ref{lem:freeend}). At a
level off the content list the cell's determinant reads unequal
members at the free end, so the free end sits inside its cell and its counts are the
cell's, direct reads; at a level on the list,
Lemma~\ref{lem:cellcount}'s boundary clause extends the
positive-semidefinite cells into the closed endpoint. The free-end reading of
Definition~\ref{def:K} is those reads.

\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[The four-point floor]\label{lem:fourpoint}
Fix two plaquettes sharing one link, the theta graph
(Construction~\ref{con:fiber}), $q_i:=\chi_\theta(U_{\partial p_i})$
the pair's modules, and the ray $[\,1:\tau^2\,]$ at a window
ground $\psi$ beyond the graph's locality band
(Lemma~\ref{lem:stableentries}). The \emph{connected four-point
read} at the pair, each field twice, is the moments' partition
fold at $\mu_{ab}:=\omega(q_1^{\,a}q_2^{\,b})$, one term per
partition of the four fields at the blocks' moment product, the
weight $j!$ at the join $j+1=b$ over the block count $b$, the
one-block term at weight one, and the side the
count's parity,
\[
\varkappa_4:=\big\langle\,
\mu_{22}+2\mu_{10}^2\mu_{02}+2\mu_{01}^2\mu_{20}
+8\mu_{10}\mu_{01}\mu_{11}
\,:\,
2\mu_{10}\mu_{12}+2\mu_{01}\mu_{21}+\mu_{20}\mu_{02}
+2\mu_{11}^2+6\mu_{10}^2\mu_{01}^2
\,\big\rangle\ .
\]
At every member the read holds a positive located floor on a cell
at the free end's side: at the \emph{pair fold}
$S:=\sum_kw_k\,[\,1:6+\hat c_2(k)\,]$ over the $\theta$-square
channel list (Construction~\ref{con:fusion}'s field;
Lemma~\ref{lem:channels} the $A$ instance) at the module--pair
weights $w_k$, the pair networks' channel norms
$[\,m_kd_k:d_\theta^{\,2}\,]$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:network}(i),(ii),
the displayed weights the $A$ instance;
Lemma~\ref{lem:blockcount}(ii)), the read sits
within the priced tail's bracket of the jet's value
\[
\hat\varkappa_4\;=\;\tau^4\,c_1^{\,2}\,
\big\langle\,S:[\,1:8\,]\,\big\rangle\ ,
\]
whose pair's side is its upper at every member, the margin at or
beyond $[\,c_1:42\,d_\theta^{\,3}\,]$.

\emph{The magnetic jet.} The ground's jet at the free end is the
displayed second-order solve (Construction~\ref{con:res}'s shape):
$\hat\psi:=1+\tau^2\psi_1+\tau^4\psi_2$ at
$\psi_1=[\,1:4\,]\sum_pq_p$, each module an eigenvector at the
plaquette content four (Proposition~\ref{prop:E0}), and $\psi_2$
the solve at $M\psi_1$'s components off the unit line: a
single-plaquette channel $\chi_k(p)$ at the content
$4\,\hat c_2(k)$, an adjacent pair's product at its channel split
$q_pq_{p'}=\sum_kF_k$ over the shared link's channels, the
contents $6+\hat c_2(k)$ and the weights
$\langle F_k,F_k\rangle=w_k$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:network}(i),(ii)),
and a distant product at eight, each component's solve the
content's cofactor.

\emph{The moments' displayed orders.} On the theta graph the
evaluation splits at the shared link and the outer word: the
shared read pairs the two loops' channels by the evaluation
identity and the outer word reads the unit label
(Theorem~\ref{thm:network}(i); Proposition~\ref{prop:wg}), so
$\mathrm{Eval}\big(f(U)\,g(V)\big)$ is the two unit coefficients'
product and the graph's free moments are the per-plaquette reads
$m_a:=\mathrm{Eval}(\chi_\theta^{\,a})$, with $m_2=1$, $m_3=c_1$
and $m_4$ the square's pairing
(Proposition~\ref{prop:repring}). A far insertion factorizes at
its own links (Lemma~\ref{lem:stableentries}): a plaquette off
the pair holds a link off the union, the union's four-cycles the
two boundaries (the incidence count, the boundary the plaquette's
own word), so the jet moments read the pair's insertions alone,
the far diagonal reads entering the pairing and the gram equally,
a summand on both members fixing the value
(Definition~\ref{def:ground}): with $T_a(k)$ the $k$-count of the
$a$-th power ($T_1(k)$ the $\theta$ read, $T_2(k)=N^k_{\theta\theta}$,
$T_2(\theta)=c_1$),
the \emph{jet moments} read
\begin{gather*}
\hat\mu_{ab}\;:=\;m_am_b
+\tau^2\,[\,1:2\,]\,\big(m_{a+1}m_b+m_am_{b+1}\big)
+\tau^4D_{ab}\ ,\\
\begin{aligned}
D_{ab}+[\,1:8\,]\,m_am_b
&=[\,1:16\,]\big(m_{a+2}m_b+2m_{a+1}m_{b+1}+m_am_{b+2}\big)\\
&\quad+[\,1:8\,]\sum_{k\ne\mathbf1}N^k_{\theta\theta}\,
\big[\,T_a(k)\,m_b+m_a\,T_b(k):\hat c_2(k)\,\big]
+T_a(\theta)\,T_b(\theta)\,S\ ,
\end{aligned}
\end{gather*}
$D_{ab}$ the join's datum over the balance pairs,
the first fold the pair insertions' square reads, the second the
single-plaquette channels' solves, and the third the pair
networks': a product state's pairing against the pair networks
reads at the $\theta$-labeled free links alone, the gram's
configuration split (Definition~\ref{def:carrier}), so
$\langle q_1^{\,a}q_2^{\,b},\,F_k\rangle
=T_a(\theta)\,T_b(\theta)\,w_k$.

\emph{The collection.} The partition fold collects the displayed
orders: the $\tau^2$ reads enter the fold's two members equally
($m_3m_2$-type terms on each side), the single-channel folds and
the $m_4$ reads enter $D_{22}$ and twice $D_{20}$ equally, and the
$\tau^2$-cross products collect against the square terms, so the
fold closes on the pair-network read against the distant
product's,
$\hat\varkappa_4=\tau^4c_1^{\,2}\,\langle S:[\,1:8\,]\rangle$,
the jet moments' partition fold at the displayed orders: the pair
solve at the shared channels' contents
against the two modules' first-order reads at eight.

\emph{The side.} The channel list's two closure identities,
$\sum_kw_k=1$ and $\sum_kw_k\,\hat c_2(k)=2$, the square's
exhaustion count and the uniform drift
(Lemma~\ref{lem:blockcount}(ii); Theorem~\ref{thm:drift};
Theorem~\ref{thm:xdata}'s displays the $A$ instances), read
$\sum_kw_k\,(6+\hat c_2(k))=8$: the product of $S$ with that
fold, against the weights' squared fold $(\sum_kw_k)^2=1$,
collects per channel pair, each pair's cleared read the square
identity
$(6+\hat c_2(k))^2+(6+\hat c_2(l))^2
=\langle\hat c_2(k)\!:\!\hat c_2(l)\rangle^2
+2\,(6+\hat c_2(k))\,(6+\hat c_2(l))$,
one display per pair, so
\[
8\,S\;=\;1+\sum_{k<l}w_k\,w_l\,
\big\langle\hat c_2(k):\hat c_2(l)\big\rangle^2\,
\big[\,1:(6+\hat c_2(k))(6+\hat c_2(l))\,\big]\ ,
\]
every summand a square at pair weights, positive by its shape at
distinct channel Casimirs, and the fold at or beyond its unit
against $\theta$ term, the unit channel at the kernel point and
weight $[\,1:d_\theta^{\,2}\,]$ against the $\theta$ channel at
one and weight $[\,c_1:d_\theta\,]$
(Proposition~\ref{prop:repring}; Proposition~\ref{prop:row}'s
dimension fold): $8S$ exceeds one at the margin
$[\,c_1:42\,d_\theta^{\,3}\,]$ or beyond, at every member.

\emph{The tail, and the cell.} The jet's identity is exact at the
displayed join,
\[
E\,\hat\psi+\tau^4\,[\,\#p:4\,]\,\mathbf1+\tau^6M\psi_2
\;=\;\tau^2M\,\hat\psi\ ,
\]
$E\psi_1=M\mathbf1$ outright (the modules' fold, its unit
coordinate the sum's unit) with $E\psi_2$ against $M\psi_1$ the
join at $[\,\#p:4\,]\,\mathbf1$, so the jet solves the pencil to
the $\tau^6$ read, and the level gap at
the free cell's clearance (Lemma~\ref{lem:freeend};
Lemma~\ref{lem:freecell}) prices the ground's gap from the jet,
the transport display's pairing step
(Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(iii)); each moment's bracket
follows at the observables' caps (Lemma~\ref{lem:loopcap}), one
polarization each, and two windows' reads sit within the
spectator brackets (Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(iii)): the
tail's fold is $\tau^6$ against displayed caps of the anchor
window, the graph's band neighborhood. The \emph{cell} is the
$\tau$-segment below the comparison's first located root
(Theorem~\ref{thm:windowsep}), the leading read against twice the
tail's fold: on it
$\varkappa_4$ reads its upper side at the margin
$[\,1:2\,]\,\tau^4c_1^{\,2}\,\langle S:[\,1:8\,]\rangle$ or
beyond, at every window beyond the band, the floor's data located
member reads.
\end{lemma}

\section{The reconstruction}

\begin{theorem}[The located reconstruction]\label{thm:reconstruct}
The passage from the Euclidean bracket families to the
relativistic ones is a theorem over the located stage. Its
inputs are the bracketed ground reads
(Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}), the restoration counts with the
cone read (Theorem~\ref{thm:restoration}; Lemma~\ref{lem:cone}),
and the pairing's definitional positivity
(Definition~\ref{def:carrier}); its outputs, per member
and direction count at every window of the corner cell:

\emph{(i) The spectral data are located divisor data.} Per fiber
the energy roots with their weights are the split's own
(Lemma~\ref{lem:split}), the Euclidean families read as the
weight folds over them (Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(v)), and
on the clearance cell the moment folds' solve returns the
truncated moment vector from the streams' reads
(Lemma~\ref{lem:momentfold}), the located divisor read its
clearance: the divisor data read the Euclidean families as
their weight folds, one elimination per stream.

\emph{(ii) The relativistic bracket families are those data at
the cone's coordinates.} Per probe pair the family is the weight
fold over the located roots at the fiber's chord, its covariance
identity reads the restoration counts' (the momentum reads
isotropic at degree two with the direction data two degrees
down, Lemma~\ref{lem:cone}), and the families are window-free
within the spectator brackets
(Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(iii)), the growth caps their
$m$-point folds' reads (Theorem~\ref{thm:groundreads}(v)).

\emph{(iii) The state reads close the families.} The pairing's
positivity is the families' own Gram read at every finite probe
list, the cut is their spectrum condition
(Lemma~\ref{lem:speccut}), the word sector is the state's space,
cyclic (Lemma~\ref{lem:statespace}), and every read holds at
every window ground at once: each output is a located bracket
read of the inputs, one solve and one count per datum.
\end{theorem}

\section{The quasi-local algebra}

\begin{definition}[The quasi-local algebra]\label{def:qla}
$\mathfrak A$ is the carrier of the infinite lattice with its filtration
by least windows: every element has one
(Proposition~\ref{prop:leastwindow}), so $\mathfrak A$ is the union of
the regions' local algebras. A winding operator exists only on the finite
torus: its index support wraps a cycle of the torus, while every
configuration of the infinite lattice's index has finite support
(Definition~\ref{def:carrier}), so winding operators lie outside every local algebra and outside
$\mathfrak A$ with them.
\end{definition}
